# Agents · S · part 46

956 entries, standup to steer-reviewer.agent. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **standup**: Standup ceremony agent — daily standup summaries and async reports. Use when the user wants to run a standup or generate a standup report. ([parkiyong/scrum-agents](https://github.com/parkiyong/scrum-agents/blob/HEAD/agents/standup.md))
- **standup-agent**: Daily async standup for teams (duo or team mode). Runs every morning at 9:00 AM when team mode is active. Produces a brief shareable summary of what each member is doing today, what's blocked, and the team's revenue + pipeline pulse. Trigger with "daily standup", "team briefing", "what is everyone working on today". NOT triggered in solo mode — monday-morning-agent handles solo briefings. ([jamon8888/cc-suite](https://github.com/jamon8888/cc-suite/blob/HEAD/solo/agents/standup-agent.md))
- **standup-bot**: Daily standup — run at the start of every coding session. Shows what shipped yesterday, any fix chains to be aware of, today's priority story, and open blockers. ([WANDERCOLTD/HF](https://github.com/WANDERCOLTD/HF/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/standup-bot.md))
- **standup-critic**: Evaluates a standup draft against the contract with a fresh context. Approves or returns specific revision notes. Does NOT rewrite the draft. ([vuongvm812/extend-claude-code](https://github.com/vuongvm812/extend-claude-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/standup-critic.md) · ★1)
- **standup-gen**: Generates a standup update (as plain bullet points, in the user's own casual voice) summarizing what was actually done in the current Claude Code session. Use this when the user asks for a standup, a status update, a recap of what they worked on, or "what did we do today", NOT proactively, only on request. Only covers work since the last standup this agent generated in the current session; if none was generated yet, covers the whole session. ([1Shubham7/agents](https://github.com/1Shubham7/agents/blob/HEAD/agents/standup-gen.md) · ★2)
- **standup-generator**: Drafts the standup summary by fetching Slack activity and writing against a provided contract. Does NOT decide what "done" means. ([vuongvm812/extend-claude-code](https://github.com/vuongvm812/extend-claude-code/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/standup-generator.md) · ★1)
- **standup-helper-prompt**: Prompt to quickly create a short standup update based on my knowledge bank. - **standup-reader**: Morning standup — reads DEVLOG, cross-references project state, suggests where to pick up ([cardinalconseils/claude-starter](https://github.com/cardinalconseils/claude-starter/blob/HEAD/legacy/agents/standup-reader.md) · ★3)
- **standup-report-agent**: Generates a terse Mode A standup draft scaffold from real GitHub and Jira/Confluence activity. Invoke for "generate a standup report", "standup for &lt;user&gt;", "what did I do last week", or "write my weekly update". Runs the generate-standup-report skill (gather.py) to collect activity as structured JSON, synthesizes a Mode A draft scaffold, and renders it per the user's preferences file (identity, workspace, output format, and output style all live there, never in this agent). The output is a structured starting point intended to be rewritten through the user's voice; human finishing is expecte… ([addisonbeck/nix](https://github.com/addisonbeck/nix/blob/HEAD/bobert/agents/standup-report-agent.md) · ★1)

- **star-schema-classifier**: AtelierBID dimensional modeller — Agent 1. Classifies every column of a flat source table into one of five Kimball roles (fact_measure, dimension_attribute, dimension_fk, degenerate_dimension, date_time) and proposes a grain. Dispatched by the model-star-schema skill; not for direct user use. ([DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier](https://github.com/DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier/blob/HEAD/agents/star-schema-classifier.md))
- **star-schema-designer**: AtelierBID dimensional modeller — Agent 2. Designs a complete Kimball-compliant star schema (fact table(s), dimensions with SK/BK/SCD, relationships, design notes) from classified columns. Honours validator feedback on retry. Dispatched by the model-star-schema skill; not for direct user use. ([DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier](https://github.com/DATA-AI-XYZ/Atelier/blob/HEAD/agents/star-schema-designer.md))
- **star-schema-validator**: AtelierBID dimensional modeller — Agent 3. Reviews a star schema against the nine Kimball rules (K-01…K-09), scores it 0–100, sets approved, and returns specific fixes. - **status-checker**: Research extraction for one restriction instrument whose status nobody has settled (an NREL-compiled siting ordinance, or a moratorium past its end date). Give it a card from `python scripts/review_packets.py card status &lt;instrument_id&gt;` and a signature. It finds the body's current code, or the minutes that extended, amended or repealed the instrument, reads it, and decides whether the instrument applies today (active, extended, pending, lifted or expired, with the end date), then writes one draft packet. Can also make small ticket-only fixes (single-file markdown edits). Code edits are out of scope — defer to python-coder / sql-coder.
